Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[WSspDocIndicator]
( /* 

	Exec WSspDocIndicator 2, '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '', '0', '', '', 1033, 1065, '3', '', '', 'AA-10-21'

   */
@Mode INT,
@DateTime DateTime, 
@FDateTime DateTime, 
@TDateTime DateTime,  
@DocNumber VARCHAR(1000), 
@CompanyID VARCHAR(2000), 
@DocOprID VARCHAR(50),
@WHId VARCHAR(50),
@WHIdI VARCHAR(50),
@OrderId VARCHAR(50),
@BuyOrderId VARCHAR(50),
@DocFirstRegisterUserId VARCHAR(50),
/*--*/
@PrdFirstGroupId VARCHAR(2000),
@PrdSecondGroupId VARCHAR(2000),
@PrdId VARCHAR(2000),
/*--*/
@VerificationWorkFlowId VARCHAR(50),
@VerificationWorkFlowStepId VARCHAR(50),
/*--*/
@OtherFieldName VARCHAR(2000),
@OtherFieldValueLikely VARCHAR(50),
@OtherFieldValue VARCHAR(2000),
/*--*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL,
@rptUserId INT , 
@rptDate VARCHAR(50), 
@rptDay VARCHAR(50),
/*NKR14000607- Start*/
@Tracing NVARCHAR(200)
/*NKR14000607- End*/
)
AS
BEGIN
/*--*/
DECLARE @Qry VARCHAR(max), @Term VARCHAR(max),@TermExtra NVARCHAR(MAX) = '',
		@QryI VARCHAR(max), @TermI VARCHAR(max) 
/*--
--*/
/*--*/
SET @Term = ' 
WHERE D.FirstRegisterUserId
IN
(
SELECT ' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(10)) + ' UserId
UNION 
SELECT LinkedUserId
FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers LU INNER JOIN dbo.Users U 
     		ON (U.UserId = LU.LinkedUserId)
WHERE LinkedTypeId = 18 /*Doc*/
AND CrmUserId = '+CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(10))
+
') ' 

SET @TermI = @Term
/*--*/
/*--*/
/*NKR14000607- Start*/
IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(@Tracing, ''))) <> '')
BEGIN
	/**/
	SET @Term = @Term + ' AND D.[DocID] IN (SELECT doci.[DocId] FROM dbo.[WSDocItems] doci WHERE doci.[Tracing] = ''' + LTRIM(RTRIM(@Tracing)) + ''')'
	SET @TermI = @Term
	/**/
END
/*NKR14000607- End*/
/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @DateTime <> ''		
BEGIN 	
	SET @Term = @Term +	
	' AND CAST(CAST(DocDateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) = ''' + CAST(CAST(@DateTime AS DATE) AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''	
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END
IF @FDateTime <> '' AND @TDateTime <> ''	
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND DocDateTime BETWEEN ''' + CAST(@FDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''' AND ''' + CAST(@TDateTime AS VARCHAR(50)) + ''''
	SET @TermI = @Term	
END
IF @DocNumber > '0'	
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND DocNumber IN ( ' + @DocNumber +')'
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END
IF @CompanyID > '0'	
BEGIN
	if @CompanyID = '-20'
	BEGIN
		DECLARE @txt VARCHAR(max)
		SET @txt = 'CRMCompany'
		SELECT @txt = TXT
		FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
		WHERE UserId = @rptUserId
		AND TypeId = 1	
		/*--*/
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND CompanyId IN (  	
		SELECT CompanyId FROM ( ' + 
		@txt
		+ ' )t )'		
	END
	ELSE
	BEGIN
		SET @Term = @Term +
		' AND CompanyId IN ( ' + @CompanyID  +')'
	END	
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END               
IF @DocOprID > '0'	
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND OprId IN( ' + @DocOprID  + ')'
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END
IF @WHId > '0'	
BEGIN
	/*SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND WHId IN( ' + @WHId  + ')'*/
	/*RZ14000324- Modify Condition */
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND D.WHId IN( ' + @WHId  + ')'
	/*RZ14000324- End */
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END
/*RZ14000227- Add WrhsUserPermission*/
ELSE 
BEGIN
	/*SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND WHId IN(SELECT WUP.[WHId]
				  FROM [dbo].[WSWareHouseUserPermissions] WUP
				  WHERE ISNULL(WUP.[CrmUserId],0) IN(0,' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(20)) + ')) '*/
	
	/*RZ14000324- Modify Condition */
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND D.WHId IN(SELECT WUP.[WHId]
				  FROM [dbo].[WSWareHouseUserPermissions] WUP
				  WHERE ISNULL(WUP.[CrmUserId],0) IN(0,' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(20)) + ')) '
	/*RZ14000324- End */
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END
/*RZ14000227- End*/
IF @WHIdI > '0'	
BEGIN
	/*SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND WHIdI IN( ' + @WHIdI  + ')'*/
	/*RZ14000324- Modify Condition */
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND D.WHIdI IN( ' + @WHIdI  + ')'
	/*RZ14000324- End */
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END
/*RZ14000227- Add WrhsUserPermission*/
ELSE 
BEGIN
	/*SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND (WHIdI IN(SELECT WUP.[WHId]
				  FROM [dbo].[WSWareHouseUserPermissions] WUP
				  WHERE ISNULL(WUP.[CrmUserId],0) IN(0,' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(20)) + ')) OR WHIdI IS NULL) '*/
	/*RZ14000324- Modify Condition */
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND (D.WHIdI IN(SELECT WUP.[WHId]
				  FROM [dbo].[WSWareHouseUserPermissions] WUP
				  WHERE ISNULL(WUP.[CrmUserId],0) IN(0,' + CAST(@rptUserId AS VARCHAR(20)) + ')) OR WHIdI IS NULL) '
	/*RZ14000324- End */
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END
/*RZ14000227- End*/

IF @OrderId > '0'	
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND OrderId IN( ' + @OrderId  + ')'
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END
IF @BuyOrderId > '0'	
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND BuyOrderId IN( ' + @BuyOrderId  + ')'
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END
IF @DocFirstRegisterUserId > '0'		
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND FirstRegisterUserId IN ( ' + @DocFirstRegisterUserId  +')'
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @Term
END 
/*--
--*/
IF @PrdFirstGroupId > '0'		
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND D.DocID IN 
	( 
	SELECT doci.DocId 
	FROM dbo.WSDocItems doci INNER JOIN dbo.WSProducts p ON doci.PrdId = p.ProductID
					INNER JOIN WSProductSecondGroup psg ON 
	p.prdgroupid = prdSecondGroupId
	WHERE psg.FirstGroupId IN( ' + @PrdFirstGroupId + '))'	
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @TermI + 
	' AND (SELECT FirstGroupId FROM dbo.WSProductSecondGroup  
	  WHERE PrdSecondGroupID = P.prdGroupId) ' + 
	'  IN ( ' +  @PrdFirstGroupId   + ')  ' 		
END
IF @PrdSecondGroupId > '0'		
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND D.DocID IN 
	( 
	SELECT doci.DocId 
	FROM dbo.WSDocItems doci INNER JOIN dbo.WSProducts p ON doci.PrdId = p.ProductID
	WHERE p.PrdGroupID IN( ' + @PrdSecondGroupId + '))'	
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @TermI + 
	' AND P.prdGroupId IN ( ' +  @PrdSecondGroupId   + ')  ' 	
END
IF @PrdId > '0'		
BEGIN
	SET @Term = @Term +
	' AND D.DocID IN 
	( 
	SELECT doci.DocId FROM dbo.WSDocItems doci
	WHERE PrdId IN( ' + @PrdId + ' ) ) ' 	
	/*--*/
	SET @TermI = @TermI + 
	' AND PrdId IN ( ' +  @PrdId   + ')  ' 	
END
/*--*/
IF @VerificationWorkFlowId > '0'		
BEGIN
	/*NKU980821*/
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +
	' AND VerificationWorkFlowId IN ( ' + @VerificationWorkFlowId  +')'
	/*--*/
	/*SET @TermI = @Term*/
	/*NKU980821-END*/
END 
IF @VerificationWorkFlowStepId > '0'		
BEGIN
	/*NKU980821*/
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra +
	' AND VerificationWorkFlowStepId IN ( ' + @VerificationWorkFlowStepId  +')'
	/*--*/
	/*SET @TermI = @Term*/
	/*NKU980821-END*/
END 
/*--OtherField*/
IF @OtherFieldName <> '0'
BEGIN
	/*NKU980821*/
	IF @OtherFieldValue = '-1'	
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra + ' AND (   REPLACE(CAST(  D.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR (1610)) '+
		' IS NULL OR D.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' = '''') '
	ELSE 
	IF @OtherFieldValue = '-2'	
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra + ' AND IsNull(    REPLACE(CAST(  D.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740),  NCHAR(1610)) '+
		' , '''') <> '''' '
	ELSE
	IF @OtherFieldValueLikely = 1
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra + ' AND     REPLACE(CAST(  D.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR (1610)) '+
		' Like (''%''+ ''' + @OtherFieldValue + ''' + ''%'')'
	ELSE 
	SET @TermExtra = @TermExtra + ' AND      REPLACE(CAST(  D.'+@OtherFieldName+ ' as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR (1610)) '+
		' = '+ '''' + @OtherFieldValue + '''' 
	/*--*/
	/*SET @TermI = @Term*/
	/*NKU980821-END*/
END
/**/
/*--
--
--*/
/*NKU980821*/
SET @Term = @Term + @TermExtra
SET @TermI = @TermI + @TermExtra
/*NKU980821-END*/
/*--
--
--*/
if (@LanguageID is null or @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @Qry = 
	' 
	SELECT  Distinct   
	DocID, 
	OprId, 
	(Select [Name] from WSDocOpr DP where DP.DocOprID = D.OprId) OprName,
	(Select OprType from WSDocOpr DP where DP.DocOprID = D.OprId) OprType,
	DocNumber, 
	DocDate, 
	DocDateTime,
	SenderId, SenderName, FactorNumber, 
	Description, IsFix, 
	WHId, 
	(SELECT WrhsName FROM WsWareHouse WH WHERE WH.WareHouseID = D.WHId) WrhsName, 
	WHIdI,
	(SELECT WrhsName FROM WsWareHouse WH WHERE WH.WareHouseID = D.WHIdI) WrhsNameI,
	FirstRegisterUserId,
	(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+ '' '' +ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM Users U WHERE U.userID = D.FirstRegisterUserId) FirstRegisterUser,
	FirstRegisterDatePersian, FirstRegisterDate,
	UserId, 
	(SELECT ISNULL(FName, '''')+ '' '' +ISNULL(LName, '''') FROM Users U WHERE U.userID = D.UserId) RegUser,
	Month1, FPId, 
	RegisterDate, RegisterDatePersian, 	 
	SendAddress, DriverName, DriverTel, FreightName, BijakNumber, CarNumber,  
	CompanyId, 
	/*IsNull(
	( SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle t WHERE CompanyTitleId = 
		(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany c WHERE c.CompanyID = D.CompanyId) ), '''') + '' '' +
	(SELECT CC.CmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC WHERE CC.CompanyID = D.CompanyId) Company,*/
	/*RZ14000620- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	(SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = D.CompanyId) Company,
	/*RZ14000620- End*/	

	cmpTitle,
	(SELECT Province FROM Province P WHERE ProvinceId = 
		(SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM dbo.CrmCompany 
		WHERE CompanyID = D.CompanyId) ) CompanyProvince, 
	(SELECT City FROM City C WHERE CityId = 
		(SELECT cmpCityId FROM dbo.CrmCompany 
		WHERE CompanyID = D.CompanyId) ) CompanyCity, 
	(SELECT CC.cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC 
		WHERE CC.CompanyID = D.CompanyId) CompanycmpAddress,	
	(SELECT CC.cmpZipCode FROM dbo.CrmCompany CC 
		WHERE CC.CompanyID = D.CompanyId) cmpZipCode,

	,(SELECT TOP 1 [bordIFCR] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI WHERE BOI.[BuyOrderId] = D.[BuyOrderId] AND BOI.[bordIPrdId] = DI.[PrdId] AND BOI.[bordIWHId] = DI.[WHId] AND BOI.[bordIQuantity] = DI.[Quantity]) [bordIFCR]
	,(SELECT TOP 1 [bordITax] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI WHERE BOI.[BuyOrderId] = D.[BuyOrderId] AND BOI.[bordIPrdId] = DI.[PrdId] AND BOI.[bordIWHId] = DI.[WHId] AND BOI.[bordIQuantity] = DI.[Quantity]) [bordITax]
	,(SELECT TOP 1 [bordITaxIndex] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI WHERE BOI.[BuyOrderId] = D.[BuyOrderId] AND BOI.[bordIPrdId] = DI.[PrdId] AND BOI.[bordIWHId] = DI.[WHId] AND BOI.[bordIQuantity] = DI.[Quantity]) [bordITaxIndex] 
	,(SELECT TOP 1 [bordIFCRI] FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems] BOI WHERE BOI.[BuyOrderId] = D.[BuyOrderId] AND BOI.[bordIPrdId] = DI.[PrdId] AND BOI.[bordIWHId] = DI.[WHId] AND BOI.[bordIQuantity] = DI.[Quantity]) [bordIFCRI]	
	 /*NKR14000607- Start*/
	 ,DI.Tracing
	 /*NKR14000607- End*/ 
	 /*RZ14000712- Add LinkAcc Fileds*/
	,[DocLinkAccCodeId]
    ,[DocLinkAccCode]
    ,[DocLinkAccDocId]
    ,[DocLinkAccDocNumber]
    ,[DocLinkAccVoucherId]
    ,[DocLinkAccVoucherNumber]      
    ,[DocLinkAccDocIdStr]
    ,[DocLinkAccDocType]
	/*RZ14000712- End*/ '
	/*RZ14010425- Add PrdUnitId*/
	SET @QryI = @QryI + ' ,DI.[PrdUnitId] [docIPrdUnitId]
		,(SELECT TOP 1 [Unit] FROm dbo.WSProductUnits_Lang PU WHERE PU.ProductUnitID = DI.[PrdUnitId] AND PU.LanguageID='+CAST(@LanguageID AS VARCHAR(MAX))+') [docIPrdUnit]  '	
	/*RZ14010426- End*/

	SET @QryI = @QryI + ' FROM dbo.WSDoc D INNER JOIN dbo.[WSDocItems] DI ON (D.[DocID] = DI.[DocId]) '

	/*RZ990630- End*/
END
/**/

/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/
IF @Mode = 1 /*Indicator*/
BEGIN
Execute ( @Qry + @Term + ' Order By DocDateTime Desc, DocNumber Desc ')	
/*--*/
DELETE CrmsysDBTempInfo 
WHERE UserId = @rptUserId
AND TypeId = 60
/*--*/
INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (
	UserId,
	TypeId,
	TXT
) VALUES ( @rptUserId, 60, @Term) 
/*--*/
END
ELSE IF @Mode = 2 /*--ListOrder*/ /*Filter Doc with Product*/
BEGIN
EXECUTE ( @QryI + @TermI  + ' ORDER BY D.CompanyId, D.DocNumber, D.DocDateTime, DI.PrdId ')	
END
/*-------------------------------------------------------------*/

END

				@TmpI NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,
				@TmpIII NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL,@TmpPrice NUMERIC(18,2) = NULL

				SET @ordITax = NULL
				SET @ordITaxSum = NULL

					 @ordIPrdId = [bordIPrdId]					
					,@ordIDescription = [bordIDescription]					
					,@ordIWHId = [bordIWHId]										
					,@ordIFCR = [bordIFCR]
					,@ordIFCRI = [bordIFCRI]					
					,@ordITracing = [bordITracing]
					,@ordIPrdUnitId = [bordIPrdUnitId]
					,@ordIQuantityTotal = [bordIQuantityTotal]
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI = [bordIPrdUnitIdI]
					,@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult = [bordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
					,@ordIPrdNickname = [bordIPrdNickname]

					,@ordINotInSum = [bordINotInSum]

				WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] = @BordItemId

				SELECT 				
					@PrdUnitIRate = [PrdUnitIRate],
					@PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto = [PrdUnitIRateCalcAuto],
					@PrdQtyNoFCR = [PrdQtyNoFCR]				
				FROM [dbo].[WSProducts]
				WHERE ProductId = @ordIPrdId
				/*------------------*/
				SELECT TOP(1)
					@ordIDiscount = [Discount],
					@ordIDiscountPercent = [DiscountPrecent]
				FROM [dbo].[WSProductPrice] PP
				WHERE PrdId = @ordIPrdId 
				AND ProductPriceGroupId = @ordIPrdPriceGroupID
				ORDER BY DocDateTime DESC,PrdPriceID DESC					
				/*------------------*/
				/*------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIQuantityTotal = @ordIQuantity * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1)
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCR,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)
				/*--------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1) 					
				/**/
				END
				/*--------------*/
				IF (ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 1)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,1) 					
					/**/
					END
					ELSE IF(ISNULL(@PrdQtyNoFCR,0) = 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @TmpI = ISNULL(@ordIQuantityTotal,1) * ISNULL(@ordIFCRI,1)					
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*--------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0) <> 0 AND ISNULL(@bordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = (1 / ISNULL(@PrdUnitIRate,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIQuantity,1)

					IF(ISNULL(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult,0) <> 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordIPrdUnitIRateResult = CAST((FORMAT(@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult, '000.000')) AS FLOAT) 
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = ((ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100
					SET @ordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100					
				/**/
				END				
				ELSE IF(ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/	
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
					IF(ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordIDiscountPercent = (ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0) * 100) / (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0))
						/*SET @ordIDiscount = (ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordIDiscountPercent,0)) / 100*/
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				SET @TmpPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscount,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)			
				SET @TmpIII = ((ISNULL(@ordIPrice,0) - ISNULL(@ordIRound,0) + ISNULL(@ordIRoundPlus,0)) * ISNULL(@TmpI,0)) - ISNULL(@ordIDiscountSum,0)	
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@ordICalculateTax,0) = 1)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					IF(ISNULL(@TmpIII,0) > 0 AND ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) > 0)
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @ordITaxSum = ((ISNULL(@TmpIII,0)) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
						SET @ordITax = (ISNULL(@TmpPrice,0) * ISNULL(@ordITaxIndex,0) / 100)
					/**/
					END
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				SET @ordIPriceSum = @TmpIII
				SET @ordITotalPrice = ISNULL(@ordIPriceSum,0) + ISNULL(@ordITaxSum,0)
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@cuUnitIsDecimal,0) = 0)
				BEGIN
					SET @ordIPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPrice))
					SET @ordIDiscount = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscount))					
					SET @ordIDiscountSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIDiscountSum))
					SET @ordITaxSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITaxSum))
					SET @ordITax = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITax))			
					SET @ordIPriceSum = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordIPriceSum))
					SET @ordITotalPrice = (SELECT [dbo].[fnRoundNumberCalculation](@ordITotalPrice))					
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/				
				SET @ItemCount = (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI WHERE OI.OrderId = @InsertedOrderID)
				SET @ordIOrderView = (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) + 1) + (ISNULL(@ItemCount,0) * 4 ) - 1
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems]
					([OrderId]
					,[ordIPrdId]
					,[ordIQuantity]
					,[ordIPrice]
					,[ordITotalPrice]
					,[ordIDescription]
					,[UserId]
					,[FPId]
					,[Month1]
					,[RegisterDate]
					,[RegisterDatePersian]
					,[ordIQuantityDesc]
					,[ordIDiscountI]
					,[ordIDiscount]
					,[ordIDiscountPercent]
					,[ordIDiscountIPercent]
					,[ordIPrdUnitId]
					,[ordICurrencyUnitId]
					,[ordIOrderView]
					,[ordIFCR]
					,[ordITax]
					,[ordITaxIndex]
					,[ordICalculateTax]
					,[ordINotVisible]
					,[ordIFCRI]
					,[ordINotInSum]
					,[ordIWHId]
					,[ordIRound]
					,[AppType]
					,[LanguageID]
					,[FirstRegisterUserId]
					,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
					,[FirstRegisterDate]
					,[ordIRoundPlus]					
					,[ordIPrdPriceGroupID]
					,[ordITracing]
					,[ordIPriceSum]
					,[ordIDiscountISum]
					,[ordIDiscountSum]
					,[ordITaxSum]					
					,[ordILinkAccWHIdStr]					
					,[ordIQuantityTotal]
					,[ordIPrdUnitIdI]
					,[ordIPrdUnitIRateResult]
					,[ordIPrdColorId]					
					,[ordIPrdNickname]															
					,[BuyOrderItemIds])
				VALUES
					(@InsertedOrderID
					,@ordIPrdId
					,@ordIQuantity
					,@ordIPrice
					,@ordITotalPrice
					,@ordIDescription
					,@UserId
					,@FPId
					,@Month1
					,@RegisterDate
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@ordIQuantityDesc
					,@ordIDiscountI
					,@ordIDiscount
					,@ordIDiscountPercent
					,@ordIDiscountIPercent					
					,@ordIPrdUnitId
					,@ordCurrencyUnitId
					,@ordIOrderView
					,@ordIFCR
					,@ordITax
					,@ordITaxIndex
					,@ordICalculateTax
					,@ordINotVisible
					,@ordIFCRI
					,@ordINotInSum
					,@ordIWHId
					,@ordIRound
					,@AppType
					,@DefaultLanguageID
					,@UserId
					,@RegisterDatePersian
					,@RegisterDate
					,@ordIRoundPlus					
					,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
					,@ordITracing					
					,@ordIPriceSum
					,@ordIDiscountISum
					,@ordIDiscountSum
					,@ordITaxSum					
					,@ordILinkAccWHIdStr
					,@ordIQuantityTotal
					,@ordIPrdUnitIdI
					,@ordIPrdUnitIRateResult
					,@ordIPrdColorId					
					,@ordIPrdNickname										
					,@BuyOrderItemIds)

				SET @OrderItemID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
				/*-------------------------------------------*/
				IF(ISNULL(@OrderItemID,0) > 0)
				BEGIN
				/**/
					INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItems_Lang]
						([OrderItemID]
						,[ordIDescription]
						,[ordIQuantityDesc]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[ordIPrdNickname] 
						)
					SELECT
						 @OrderItemID
						,[bordIDescription]
						,[bordIQuantityDesc]
						,[LanguageID]
						,[bordIPrdNickname] 
					FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItems_Lang]
					WHERE [BuyOrderItemId] = @BordItemId
					AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID					
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
					DECLARE @OrderItemSerialID INT = NULL,@SerialId INT = NULL,
					@OrdItemSerialDesc NVARCHAR(300) = NULL,@OrdItemSerialWHId INT = NULL,
					@DocItemSerialID INT = NULL	,@BuyOrderItemSerialID INT = NULL
					/*------------ Create tmp table for serial ------*/
					DECLARE @Term VARCHAR(MAX) ='', @Qry VARCHAR(MAX) = ''
					DECLARE @Tmp_BordItemSerial TABLE(
						 [BuyOrderItemSerialID] INT
						,[Serial] NVARCHAR(50)
						,[Description] NVARCHAR(300)
						,[WHId] INT
						,[SerialId] INT										
					)
					DELETE FROM @Tmp_BordItemSerial
					/*--------------------*/
					IF(@OprType = 1) /*sale*/
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @Qry = N'
							SELECT 
	 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
	,[Serial]
	,[Description]
	,[WHId]
	,[SerialId]
FROM(
	SELECT  DISTINCT TOP ((CAST(' + CAST(@ordIQuantity AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AS INT))) 
		 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
		,[Serial]
		,[Description]
		,[WHId]
	   ,(SELECT TOP(1) [DocItemSerialID]
		FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
		WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
						   FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
						   WHERE PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
						   AND DI.BuyOrderItemId = BIS.BuyOrderItemId
						   AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
										  FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
										  WHERE D.BuyOrderId = ' + CAST(@BuyOrderID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')	
						   AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(DIS.Serial,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
							   = (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((BIS.Serial),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
							)								
	
		) SerialId    
    
	FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials] BIS 
	WHERE BIS.[BuyOrderItemId] = ' + CAST(@bordItemId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '  
	AND ISNULL(Serial,'''') <> ''''
	AND  (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((BIS.Serial),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
												IN(SELECT (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(DIS.Serial,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
														FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
														WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																			FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
																			WHERE PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
																			AND DI.BuyOrderItemId = BIS.BuyOrderItemId
																			AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																							FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																							WHERE D.BuyOrderId = ' + CAST(@BuyOrderID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
																							/*AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 1)*/)																
																			)																
													)
) AS TmpSerial '
						
						SET @Qry = @Qry + ' WHERE ISNULL([SerialId],0) > 0	
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) NOT IN(SELECT ISNULL(SerialId,0) 
															FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
															WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN (SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
																						WHERE OI.OrderId IN (SELECT O.OrderID 
																											FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																											WHERE O.OrderOprID IN (SELECT Opr.OrderOprID     
																																	FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr
																																	WHERE [OprType] = ' + CAST(@OprType AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '))))
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) NOT IN(SELECT 
															DIS.[DocItemSerialID]
														FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS 
														INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI ON(DIS.ItemId = DI.DocItemID)
														INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D ON(DI.DocId = D.DocID)
														WHERE D.WHId = TmpSerial.WHId			
														AND DI.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
														AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType IN(3))	/*IN(1,3)*/							
														AND DIS.DocItemSerialID NOT IN (SELECT ISNULL(SerialId,0) 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
																						WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN (SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
																												  FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
																												  WHERE OI.OrderId IN (SELECT O.OrderID 
																																	   FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																																	   WHERE O.OrderOprID IN (SELECT Opr.OrderOprID     
																																							  FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr
																																							  WHERE [OprType] = ' + CAST(@OprType AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')))														 
																					)			
														AND DIS.DocItemSerialID NOT IN (SELECT Isnull(DIS_I.SerialId, 0)
																						FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS_I 
																						INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI_I ON(DIS_I.ItemId = DI_I.DocItemID)
																						INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D_I ON(DI_I.DocId = D_I.DocID)
																						WHERE D_I.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType = 3)
																						And DI_I.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '	 															
																						And D_I.WHIdI = TmpSerial.WHId	 
																						UNION
																						SELECT Isnull(DIS_II.SerialId, 0)
																						FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS_II 
																						INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI_II ON(DIS_II.ItemId = DI_II.DocItemID)
																						INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D_II ON(DI_II.DocId = D_II.DocID)
																						WHERE D_II.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType = 2)
																						And DI_II.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 																
																						And D_II.WHId = TmpSerial.WHId 
																						)
							)
												'						
					/**/
					END
					ELSE IF(@OprType = 2) /*Return from sale*/
					BEGIN
					/**/
						SET @Qry = N'
							SELECT 
							 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
							,[Serial]
							,[Description]
							,[WHId]
							,[SerialId]
						FROM(
							SELECT  DISTINCT TOP((CAST(' + CAST(@ordIQuantity AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' AS INT)))
								 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
								,[Serial]
								,[Description]
								,[WHId]
							   ,(SELECT TOP(1) [DocItemSerialID]
								FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
								WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
												   FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
												   WHERE PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
												   AND DI.BuyOrderItemId = BIS.BuyOrderItemId
												   AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																  FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																  WHERE D.BuyOrderId = ' + CAST(@BuyOrderID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' )	
												   AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(DIS.Serial,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
													   = (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((BIS.Serial),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
													)								
	
								) SerialId    
    
							FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials] BIS 
							WHERE BIS.[BuyOrderItemId] = ' + CAST(@bordItemId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '  
							AND ISNULL(Serial,'''') <> ''''
							AND  (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((BIS.Serial),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) 
																		IN(SELECT (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL(DIS.Serial,'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))
																				FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS
																				WHERE [ItemId] IN (SELECT [DocItemID]    
																									FROM [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI
																									WHERE PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
																									AND DI.BuyOrderItemId = BIS.BuyOrderItemId
																									AND [DocId] IN(SELECT D.DocId 
																													FROM [dbo].[WSDoc] D 
																													WHERE D.BuyOrderId = ' + CAST(@BuyOrderID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 
																													/*AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM dbo.WSDocOpr WHERE OprType = 1)*/)																
																									)																
																			)
						) AS TmpSerial ' 
						SET @Qry = @Qry + ' WHERE ISNULL([SerialId],0) > 0	
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) NOT IN(SELECT ISNULL(SerialId,0) 
															FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
															WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN (SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
																						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
																						WHERE OI.OrderId IN (SELECT O.OrderID 
																											FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																											WHERE O.OrderOprID IN (SELECT Opr.OrderOprID     
																																	FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr
																																	WHERE [OprType] = ' + CAST(@OprType AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')))) 
						AND ISNULL([SerialId],0) IN(SELECT 
														DIS.[DocItemSerialID]						
													FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS 
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI ON(DIS.ItemId = DI.DocItemID)
													INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D ON(DI.DocId = D.DocID)
													WHERE 
														D.WHId = TmpSerial.WHId			
														AND DI.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 			
														AND D.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType IN(3))/*IN(1,3)*/
														AND DIS.DocItemSerialID NOT IN (SELECT SerialId
																						FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials] OIS
																						WHERE OIS.OrderItemId IN (SELECT OI.OrderItemId 
																												  FROM [dbo].[WSOrderItems] OI
																												  WHERE OI.OrderId IN (SELECT O.OrderID 
																																	   FROM [dbo].[WSOrder] O 
																																	   WHERE O.OrderOprID IN (SELECT Opr.OrderOprID     
																																								FROM [dbo].[WSOrderOpr] Opr
																																								WHERE [OprType] = ' + CAST(@OprType AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '))))
			
														AND DIS.DocItemSerialID IN (SELECT Isnull(DIS_I.SerialId, 0)
																					FROM [dbo].[WSDocItemSerials] DIS_I 
																					INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDocItems] DI_I ON(DIS.ItemId = DI.DocItemID)
																					INNER JOIN [dbo].[WSDoc] D_I ON(DI.DocId = D.DocID)
																					WHERE D_I.OprId IN (SELECT DocOprID FROM [dbo].[WSDocOpr] WHERE OprType = 2)
																					AND DI.PrdId = ' + CAST(@ordIPrdId AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ' 				
																					AND D.WHId = TmpSerial.WHId	 
																					)
							)
						
						'						
					/**/
					END

					INSERT INTO @Tmp_BordItemSerial ([BuyOrderItemSerialID],[Serial],[Description],[WHId],[SerialId])
					EXECUTE(@Qry)
					/*-------------------------------------------*/

					DECLARE CrsrORDItemSerial CURSOR LOCAL FOR
					/**/	
						SELECT TOP (CAST(@ordIQuantity AS INT))
							 [BuyOrderItemSerialID]
							,[Description]
							,[WHId]
							,[SerialId]
						FROM @Tmp_BordItemSerial
					/**/
					OPEN CrsrORDItemSerial
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrORDItemSerial INTO @BuyOrderItemSerialID,@OrdItemSerialDesc,@OrdItemSerialWHId,@DocItemSerialID
					WHILE @@Fetch_Status = 0
					BEGIN
					/**/
						/*-----------------------------------------------------*/
						SET @OrderItemSerialID = NULL
						INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials]
							([OrderItemId]
							,[SerialId]
							,[Description]
							,[UserId]
							,[Month1]
							,[FPId]
							,[RegisterDate]
							,[RegisterDatePersian]							
							,[WHId]
							,[AppType]
							,[LanguageID]
							,[FirstRegisterUserId]
							,[FirstRegisterDatePersian]
							,[FirstRegisterDate])
						VALUES
							(@OrderItemId
							,@DocItemSerialID
							,@OrdItemSerialDesc
							,@UserId
							,@Month1
							,@FPId
							,@RegisterDate
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@OrdItemSerialWHId
							,@AppType
							,@DefaultLanguageID
							,@UserId
							,@RegisterDatePersian
							,@RegisterDate)
						SET @OrderItemSerialID = SCOPE_IDENTITY()
						/*-------------------------------------------*/
						IF(ISNULL(@OrderItemSerialID,0) > 0 )
						BEGIN
						/**/
							INSERT INTO [dbo].[WSOrderItemSerials_Lang]
								([OrderItemSerialID]
								,[LanguageID]
								,[Description])
							SELECT
								 @OrderItemSerialID
								,[LanguageID]
								,[Description]
							FROM [dbo].[WSBuyOrderItemSerials_Lang]
							WHERE BuyOrderItemSerialID = @BuyOrderItemSerialID
							AND LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID							
						/**/
						END
						/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
					/**/	
					FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrORDItemSerial INTO @BuyOrderItemSerialID,@OrdItemSerialDesc,@OrdItemSerialWHId,@DocItemSerialID
					END
					CLOSE CrsrORDItemSerial
					DEALLOCATE CrsrORDItemSerial
					/*-------------------------------------------*/
				/**/
				END
				/*-------------------------------------------*/							
				/*-----------------------------------------------------*/														    				 					
			/**/	
			FETCH NEXT FROM CrsrOrdItem INTO @BordItemId,@ordIQuantity,@ordIPrice,@ordIPrdPriceGroupID
			END
			CLOSE CrsrOrdItem
			DEALLOCATE CrsrOrdItem
		/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
		IF (@@ERROR > 0)
		BEGIN 
			SET @ErrorFrom='Insert Into WSOrdersItems' 
			SET @ErrorNumber=@@ERROR 	
			SET @InsertedOrderID = 0
			SET @InsertedOrderNumber = 0
			ROLLBACK TRAN 			
		END		
		ELSE 
		COMMIT TRAN
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
		IF(ISNULL(@InsertedOrderID,0) > 0)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			EXEC [dbo].[WSspCalcAndUpdateOrderAmounts]
			@OrderId = @InsertedOrderID,
			@Month1 = @Month1,
			@LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID,
			@DefaultLanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID						
			/**/
			SELECT 1 AS 'Result'
		/**/
		END		
		/*--------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END

	/*------------------- tran2 --------------------*/
	/*----------------------------------
